## Data ownership audit
Texture converter has no owned data sections. NWC24MsgCommit owns 904/904 exact data bytes and kbd_lib owns 5296/5296 exact data bytes at baseline; no symbol correction is needed. zprepare owns a 48-byte, 12-entry compiler-generated jump table. Target .text offsets 0x1ce/0x1d2 reference jumptable_8166B3D0; source offsets 0x1be/0x1c2 reference @230. Both tables contain twelve relocations to Zi8PrepareMatch, with source addends 0x10 earlier than target. This is a code-layout gap, not a named source object; no rename or extent change made. extab is exact and extabindex references the non-exact function extent.

## Residual structural findings
TMCJPEGDEC_set_converterY8U8V8: target retains the shared converter-buffer base before its switch; source folds it into individual member addresses and loses one instruction. Helper extraction and local converter pointer variants did not fix it.
CheckMsgBoxSpace: equal instruction counts but different scheduling/allocation in the compiler-unrolled attachment-size estimates; signed estimates and expression regrouping regress.
kbd_led_handler: target has a 25-instruction success/error branch and tail callback; switch and if/else variants yield 22-24 instructions or a different branch shape.
KBDSetLedsAsync and KBDSetLeds: allocation, induction and inline channel-lookup boundaries differ; the target repeats channel arithmetic at the reservation and transfer boundaries. Signedness, narrowed indices and flag caching did not reach exact code.
Zi8PrepareMatch: target initializes a component-presence byte and stores it after recognizing a component, but never loads that byte. No dummy local was introduced to reproduce those dead stores. Existing loop and pointer-temporary schedules also differ.
Other remaining converter and keyboard diffs are instruction-count-equal register allocation or scheduling residuals. NWC24CommitMsgInternal retains swapped flag/buffer registers inside the registration/delay writers. WriteMIMEAttachHeader retains different live-range coalescing and addition order around its capacity estimate.
The instruction-exact gate counts fewer functions than objdiff for the converter and keyboard units at baseline; this discrepancy is unchanged and no tooling was altered.

## fz15e keyboard LED storage diagnosis
Target callback loads callback first, then stores command device zero, and compares callback with zero; current guard compares it to a read of the device field. Target asynchronous slot loop uses unsigned index, byte offset, CTR loop and indexed word loads/stores. Synchronous loop has the same CTR shape. Existing custom KBDLEDCommand layout is 0x20 but loses SDK USBKBDCmdLEDAsync base.device and base.ledBits volatility. SDK header revolution/usbkbd.h explicitly declares those fields vu32/vu8; using that actual type is authorized definition-level volatility evidence, not a use-site cast. Test native SDK record and ordinary callback-null guard.
